### [117] [Sequential Counterfactual Inference for Temporal Clinical Data: Addressing the Time Traveler Dilemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21168)
*Jingya Cheng,Alaleh Azhir,Jiazi Tian,Hossein Estiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了序列反事实框架，用于处理纵向临床数据中的时间依赖性，区分不可变特征和可控特征，并模拟干预随时间传播，应用于COVID-19患者分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准反事实推理方法假设特征独立和同时可修改性，这些假设在纵向临床数据中被违反。临床数据具有时间依赖性，需要区分慢性诊断（不可变）和实验室值（可控），并考虑干预的时间传播效应。

Method: 引入序列反事实框架，尊重电子健康记录中的时间依赖性，区分不可变特征（慢性诊断）和可控特征（实验室值），建模干预如何随时间传播。应用于2,723名COVID-19患者（383名长新冠心力衰竭病例，2,340名匹配对照）。

Result: 发现38-67%患有慢性疾病的患者在朴素方法下需要生物学上不可能的反事实。识别出心肾级联（CKD -> AKI -> HF），每一步的相对风险分别为2.27和1.19，展示了序列反事实能够捕捉而朴素方法无法捕捉的时间传播效应。

Conclusion: 该框架将反事实解释从"如果这个特征不同会怎样？"转变为"如果我们更早干预会怎样，以及这将如何向前传播？"，产生基于生物学合理性的临床可操作见解。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference enables clinicians to ask "what if" questions about patient outcomes, but standard methods assume feature independence and simultaneous modifiability -- assumptions violated by longitudinal clinical data. We introduce the Sequential Counterfactual Framework, which respects temporal dependencies in electronic health records by distinguishing immutable features (chronic diagnoses) from controllable features (lab values) and modeling how interventions propagate through time. Applied to 2,723 COVID-19 patients (383 Long COVID heart failure cases, 2,340 matched controls), we demonstrate that 38-67% of patients with chronic conditions would require biologically impossible counterfactuals under naive methods. We identify a cardiorenal cascade (CKD -> AKI -> HF) with relative risks of 2.27 and 1.19 at each step, illustrating temporal propagation that sequential -- but not naive -- counterfactuals can capture. Our framework transforms counterfactual explanation from "what if this feature were different?" to "what if we had intervened earlier, and how would that propagate forward?" --  yielding clinically actionable insights grounded in biological plausibility.

</details>


### [118] [The Diffusion Duality, Chapter II: $Ψ$-Samplers and Efficient Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21185)
*Justin Deschenaux,Caglar Gulcehre,Subham Sekhar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用于离散扩散模型的预测器-校正器采样器家族，在Uniform-state扩散中超越祖先采样，采样质量随步数增加持续提升，挑战了Masked扩散是语言建模未来的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uniform-state离散扩散模型在少步生成和引导方面表现出色，但使用祖先采样器时，采样质量随步数增加会达到平台期。需要开发能持续改进的采样方法，同时质疑Masked扩散作为扩散语言建模必然选择的假设。

Method: 1. 提出预测器-校正器采样器家族，适用于任意噪声过程，推广了先前方法；2. 与Uniform-state扩散结合使用；3. 开发高斯松弛训练阶段的内存高效课程，相比Duo减少25%训练时间和33%内存。

Result: 1. 在OpenWebText上获得更低的生成困惑度（匹配单字熵）；2. 在CIFAR10上获得更好的FID/IS分数；3. 关键优势：PC方法随采样步数增加持续改进，而传统采样器会达到平台期；4. 训练效率提升：保持OpenWebText和LM1B上可比困惑度及强下游性能。

Conclusion: 预测器-校正器采样器显著提升Uniform-state离散扩散模型的采样质量，挑战了Masked扩散是扩散语言建模必然未来的假设。同时提出的高效训练课程减少了计算成本，使方法更具实用性。

Abstract: Uniform-state discrete diffusion models excel at few-step generation and guidance due to their ability to self-correct, making them preferred over autoregressive or Masked diffusion models in these settings. However, their sampling quality plateaus with ancestral samplers as the number of steps increases. We introduce a family of Predictor-Corrector (PC) samplers for discrete diffusion that generalize prior methods and apply to arbitrary noise processes. When paired with uniform-state diffusion, our samplers outperform ancestral sampling on both language and image modeling, achieving lower generative perplexity at matched unigram entropy on OpenWebText and better FID/IS scores on CIFAR10. Crucially, unlike conventional samplers, our PC methods continue to improve with more sampling steps. Taken together, these findings call into question the assumption that Masked diffusion is the inevitable future of diffusion-based language modeling. Beyond sampling, we develop a memory-efficient curriculum for the Gaussian relaxation training phase, reducing training time by 25% and memory by 33% compared to Duo while maintaining comparable perplexity on OpenWebText and LM1B and strong downstream performance. We release code, checkpoints, and a video-tutorial on: https://s-sahoo.com/duo-ch2

</details>


### [119] [Why Pass@k Optimization Can Degrade Pass@1: Prompt Interference in LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21189)
*Anas Barakat,Souradip Chakraborty,Khushbu Pahwa,Amrit Singh Bedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析了pass@k优化导致pass@1下降的权衡问题，揭示了其理论根源在于梯度冲突和提示干扰


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pass@k是验证性大语言模型任务的常用性能指标，但现有方法在优化pass@k时会导致pass@1下降，这种权衡在实际应用中很重要，因为pass@1通常受到延迟、成本、验证器覆盖范围等操作约束的限制

Method: 通过理论分析pass@k策略优化的梯度冲突机制，研究提示干扰如何导致pass@k更新方向与pass@1方向偏离，并在数学推理任务上进行大语言模型实验验证

Result: 发现pass@k优化会隐式地对低成功率提示进行重加权，当这些提示存在负向干扰时，它们的权重增加会使pass@k更新方向偏离pass@1方向，导致pass@1性能下降

Conclusion: 揭示了pass@k与pass@1权衡的理论机制，为设计同时优化两个指标的推理感知微调方法提供了理论基础

Abstract: Pass@k is a widely used performance metric for verifiable large language model tasks, including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and short-answer reasoning. It defines success if any of $k$ independently sampled solutions passes a verifier. This multi-sample inference metric has motivated inference-aware fine-tuning methods that directly optimize pass@$k$. However, prior work reports a recurring trade-off: pass@k improves while pass@1 degrades under such methods. This trade-off is practically important because pass@1 often remains a hard operational constraint due to latency and cost budgets, imperfect verifier coverage, and the need for a reliable single-shot fallback. We study the origin of this trade-off and provide a theoretical characterization of when pass@k policy optimization can reduce pass@1 through gradient conflict induced by prompt interference. We show that pass@$k$ policy gradients can conflict with pass@1 gradients because pass@$k$ optimization implicitly reweights prompts toward low-success prompts; when these prompts are what we term negatively interfering, their upweighting can rotate the pass@k update direction away from the pass@1 direction. We illustrate our theoretical findings with large language model experiments on verifiable mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Statistical Query Lower Bounds for Smoothed Agnostic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21191)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了平滑不可知学习半空间的复杂度，给出了接近最优的SQ下界d^{Ω(1/σ²+log(1/ε))}，与已知上界d^{\tilde{O}(1/σ²)log(1/ε)}基本匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平滑不可知学习半空间问题的计算复杂度，特别是针对现有基于L1多项式回归的上界d^{\tilde{O}(1/σ²)log(1/ε)}，需要探究其是否接近最优。

Method: 使用统计查询(SQ)下界证明技术，通过线性规划对偶找到矩匹配的困难分布，该对偶问题等价于寻找目标函数平滑版本的低阶近似多项式。

Result: 证明了任何SQ算法用于平滑不可知学习半空间都需要复杂度d^{Ω(1/σ²+log(1/ε))}，这是该任务的第一个非平凡下界，与已知上界基本匹配。

Conclusion: 对于半空间的平滑不可知学习，应用L1多项式回归到函数的平滑版本本质上是最优的，计算复杂度指数依赖于平滑参数σ和误差精度ε。

Abstract: We study the complexity of smoothed agnostic learning, recently introduced by~\cite{CKKMS24}, in which the learner competes with the best classifier in a target class under slight Gaussian perturbations of the inputs. Specifically, we focus on the prototypical task of agnostically learning halfspaces under subgaussian distributions in the smoothed model. The best known upper bound for this problem relies on $L_1$-polynomial regression and has complexity $d^{\tilde{O}(1/σ^2) \log(1/ε)}$, where $σ$ is the smoothing parameter and $ε$ is the excess error. Our main result is a Statistical Query (SQ) lower bound providing formal evidence that this upper bound is close to best possible. In more detail, we show that (even for Gaussian marginals) any SQ algorithm for smoothed agnostic learning of halfspaces requires complexity $d^{Ω(1/σ^{2}+\log(1/ε))}$. This is the first non-trivial lower bound on the complexity of this task and nearly matches the known upper bound. Roughly speaking, we show that applying $L_1$-polynomial regression to a smoothed version of the function is essentially best possible. Our techniques involve finding a moment-matching hard distribution by way of linear programming duality. This dual program corresponds exactly to finding a low-degree approximating polynomial to the smoothed version of the target function (which turns out to be the same condition required for the $L_1$-polynomial regression to work). Our explicit SQ lower bound then comes from proving lower bounds on this approximation degree for the class of halfspaces.

</details>


### [121] [Untied Ulysses: Memory-Efficient Context Parallelism via Headwise Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21196)
*Ravi Ghadia,Maksim Abraham,Sergei Vorobyov,Max Ryabinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UPipe是一种在注意力头级别进行细粒度分块的新型上下文并行技术，显著减少自注意力层的激活内存使用，支持更长的上下文长度，同时保持训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文并行方法（如Ring Attention、DeepSpeed Ulysses）主要关注计算扩展，但内存效率不足，限制了支持的序列长度。更先进的技术（如完全流水线分布式Transformer）虽然能扩展上下文长度，但会降低训练吞吐量。

Method: 提出UPipe技术，在注意力头级别进行细粒度分块，显著减少自注意力层的中间张量内存使用。该方法通过精细的内存管理打破激活内存障碍，同时保持与先前上下文并行技术相当的训练速度。

Result: 对于32B Transformer模型，UPipe将注意力层的中间张量内存使用减少高达87.5%。在单节点8×H100上训练Llama3-8B时，可支持500万token的上下文长度，比先前方法提升超过25%。

Conclusion: UPipe通过注意力头级别的细粒度分块，在保持训练速度的同时显著降低内存使用，有效解决了长序列处理中的内存瓶颈问题，为超长上下文Transformer训练提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficiently processing long sequences with Transformer models usually requires splitting the computations across accelerators via context parallelism. The dominant approaches in this family of methods, such as Ring Attention or DeepSpeed Ulysses, enable scaling over the context dimension but do not focus on memory efficiency, which limits the sequence lengths they can support. More advanced techniques, such as Fully Pipelined Distributed Transformer or activation offloading, can further extend the possible context length at the cost of training throughput. In this paper, we present UPipe, a simple yet effective context parallelism technique that performs fine-grained chunking at the attention head level. This technique significantly reduces the activation memory usage of self-attention, breaking the activation memory barrier and unlocking much longer context lengths. Our approach reduces intermediate tensor memory usage in the attention layer by as much as 87.5$\%$ for 32B Transformers, while matching previous context parallelism techniques in terms of training speed. UPipe can support the context length of 5M tokens when training Llama3-8B on a single 8$\times$H100 node, improving upon prior methods by over 25$\%$.

</details>


### [122] [Test-Time Training with KV Binding Is Secretly Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21204)
*Junchen Liu,Sven Elflein,Or Litany,Zan Gojcic,Ruilong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTT的KV绑定作为序列建模层通常被解释为在线元学习，但分析显示这与记忆化解释矛盾。研究发现TTT架构可表达为学习型线性注意力算子，这解释了模型行为、支持架构简化、并行化，并将多种TTT变体统一为线性注意力形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统将测试时训练(TTT)中的KV绑定解释为在线元学习，通过记忆键值映射来适应测试数据。但作者观察到多个现象与这种记忆化解释相矛盾，因此需要重新审视TTT的数学本质和理论基础。

Method: 重新形式化TTT，证明一大类TTT架构可以表达为学习型线性注意力算子。基于这一视角，作者提出了架构简化方法、完全并行化方案，并将多种TTT变体系统性地约简为标准线性注意力形式。

Result: 新视角成功解释了先前令人困惑的模型行为，实现了性能保持下的效率提升，并为不同TTT变体提供了统一的理论框架。实验表明并行化方案在保持性能的同时显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: TTT不应被理解为测试时记忆化，而应视为具有增强表示能力的学习型线性注意力。这一重构为TTT提供了更坚实的理论基础，并带来了架构简化、并行化和统一理解的实际好处。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) with KV binding as sequence modeling layer is commonly interpreted as a form of online meta-learning that memorizes a key-value mapping at test time. However, our analysis reveals multiple phenomena that contradict this memorization-based interpretation. Motivated by these findings, we revisit the formulation of TTT and show that a broad class of TTT architectures can be expressed as a form of learned linear attention operator. Beyond explaining previously puzzling model behaviors, this perspective yields multiple practical benefits: it enables principled architectural simplifications, admits fully parallel formulations that preserve performance while improving efficiency, and provides a systematic reduction of diverse TTT variants to a standard linear attention form. Overall, our results reframe TTT not as test-time memorization, but as learned linear attention with enhanced representational capacity.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [A unified solution framework for truck-and-drone routing problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20310)
*Ke Xu,John Gunnar Carlsson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun算法的统一三阶段求解框架，用于解决卡车-无人机协同路由问题家族，在FSTSP、TSP-mD和VRP-D等多个变体上验证了其灵活性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车-无人机协同路由在最后一公里配送中应用日益广泛，但现有研究中结构框架或操作特征的微小变化就会产生完全不同的变体问题，需要设计专门的算法。为了在保持结构有效性的同时适应多种操作特征，需要统一的解决方案框架。

Method: 提出基于Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun算法的统一三阶段求解框架。该框架能够处理卡车-无人机路由问题的广泛家族，通过三个阶段的协调优化来解决不同变体问题。

Result: 在三个问题变体上进行了数值实验：FSTSP、TSP-mD和VRP-D。计算结果表明，该框架在小规模实例上能够接近最优解，在多个中等规模实例上甚至改进了已知最佳解。

Conclusion: 提出的统一三阶段框架具有灵活性和有效性，能够解决卡车-无人机协同路由问题的多个变体。框架的扩展性进一步突出了其多功能性，为这一领域提供了通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Coordinated truck-and-drone routing integrates the high capacity and range of ground vehicles with the flexible routing and speed of drones, enabling simultaneous service. Increasingly applied in last-mile delivery, this synchronization helps reduce completion time and operational costs. To improve its efficiency, various coordination modes between trucks and drones have been proposed. Each mode accommodates diverse operational constraints tailored to particular delivery requirements. In existing work, a slight change in the structural framework or operational characteristics could generate a totally different problem variant, which often requires the design of specialized algorithms. Consequently, under the requirement of maintaining structural validation and adapting to multiple operational features, this paper presents a unified three-phase solution framework based on the Lin-Kernighan-Helsgaun algorithm to solve a wide family of truck-and-drone routing problems. To validate its flexibility and effectiveness, we carry out numerical experiments on three problem variants: the Flying Sidekick Traveling Salesman Problem (FSTSP), the Traveling Salesman Problem with Multiple Drones (TSP-mD), and the Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones (VRP-D), benchmarking each against an effective algorithm. Computational results show that the framework can closely match optimal solutions on small-size instances and even improve the best-known solutions for several medium-size instances. Moreover, additional extensions are discussed to further highlight its versatility.

</details>


### [124] [Poisson Hamiltonian Pontryagin Dynamics and Optimal Control of Mechanical Systems on Lie Groupoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20326)
*Ghorbanali Haghighatdoost*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出李群胚上力学系统最优控制的泊松哈密顿Pontryagin动力学框架，证明辛叶（而非余伴随轨道）是该动力学自然的约化相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究李群胚上力学系统最优控制问题的几何结构，建立泊松哈密顿Pontryagin动力学框架，探索约化相空间的自然选择问题。

Method: 在李群胚上发展泊松哈密顿Pontryagin动力学公式，利用对偶李代数胚的典型线性泊松结构，在辛叶上描述约化动力学。

Result: 证明辛叶（而非余伴随轨道）是李群胚上Pontryagin动力学的自然约化相空间；在正则性假设下，证明了最优控制变分公式与泊松哈密顿Pontryagin系统的等价性；群胚不变拉格朗日量导致欧拉-庞加莱型约化最优性条件。

Conclusion: 建立了李群胚上力学系统最优控制的泊松哈密顿Pontryagin动力学框架，辛叶是该框架的自然约化相空间，为具有构型依赖惯性和局部对称性的系统提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a Poisson Hamiltonian formulation of Pontryagin dynamics for optimal control of mechanical systems on Lie groupoids. The reduced dynamics is formulated intrinsically on the dual Lie algebroid endowed with its canonical linear Poisson structure and evolves on its symplectic leaves. The main result of this work shows that symplectic leaves, rather than coadjoint orbits, provide the natural reduced phase spaces for Pontryagin dynamics on Lie groupoids. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we prove the equivalence between the variational formulation of the optimal control problem and the associated Poisson Hamiltonian Pontryagin system, and we show that groupoid invariant Lagrangians lead to reduced optimality conditions of Euler Poincare type. Several mechanical examples, including systems with configuration dependent inertia and local symmetries, are presented to illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [125] [A variance reduced framework for (non)smooth nonconvex-nonconcave stochastic minimax problems with extended Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20357)
*Muhammad Khan,Yangyang Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出首个统一框架处理具有弱凸性和扩展KL性质的随机约束极小极大优化问题，在非凸非凹结构下实现最先进的样本复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中的随机约束极小极大优化问题具有非凸非凹结构，现有方法通常需要强凹性或限制性几何假设，缺乏可靠高效算法

Method: 提出方差缩减算法，仅假设原始变量弱凸性和对偶变量的扩展Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz性质，适用于大规模应用

Result: 在光滑有限和设置中样本复杂度为O(√N·ε^{-max{4θ,2}})，在线光滑设置中为O(ε^{-max{6θ,3}})，非光滑问题也有相应复杂度保证

Conclusion: 这是首个统一框架，同时容纳弱凸性、扩展KL性质和方差缩减随机更新，显著扩展了可处理问题类别

Abstract: In this paper, we study stochastic constrained minimax optimization problems with nonconvex-nonconcave structure, a central problem in modern machine learning, for which reliable and efficient algorithms remain largely unexplored due to its inherent challenges. Prior approaches for nonconvex minimax optimization often require (strong) concavity on the maximization part, or certain restrictive geometric assumptions on the joint objective to have guaranteed convergence. In contrast, our method only assumes weak convexity in the primal variable and the extended Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, with exponent $θ\in [0,1]$, in the dual variable, significantly broadening the class of tractable problems. To this end, we propose a variance reduced algorithm that provably handles this general setting and achieves an $\varepsilon$-stationary solution with state-of-the-art sample complexity: in the smooth finite-sum setting, the sample complexity is $\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{N}\,\varepsilon^{-\max\{4θ,2\}}\right)$, where $N$ is the number of total samples, and in the online smooth setting, it is $\mathcal{O}\Big(\varepsilon^{-\max\{6θ,3\}}\Big)$. For the structured nonsmooth problem, the sample complexity is $\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{N}\,\max\Big\{\varepsilon^{-3}, \varepsilon^{-5θ}, \varepsilon^{-\frac{11θ-3}{2θ}}\Big\}\right)$ and $\mathcal{O}\left(\max\left\{\varepsilon^{-4}, \varepsilon^{-\frac{15θ-1}{2}}, \varepsilon^{-\frac{31θ-9}{4θ}}\right\}\right)$ respectively for the two settings. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first unified framework that jointly accommodates weak convexity, the extended KL property, and variance-reduced stochastic updates, making it highly suitable for large-scale applications.

</details>


### [126] [Stochastic Control Problems with Infinite Horizon and Regime Switching Arising in Optimal Liquidation with Semimartingale Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20552)
*Xinman Cheng,Guanxing Fu,Xiaonyu Xia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究无限时域下具有半鞅策略、随机系数和状态转换的最优控制问题，通过三组无限时域BSDE刻画值函数和最优策略，其中一组线性BSDE具有无界系数且为文献中首次出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限时域下具有复杂特征（半鞅策略、随机系数、状态转换）的最优控制问题，这类问题在金融数学和随机控制理论中具有重要应用，但现有文献缺乏对相关BSDE系统（特别是具有无界系数的线性BSDE）的系统性分析。

Method: 1. 建立三组无限时域BSDE系统（包括具有无界系数的线性BSDE）来刻画值函数和最优策略；2. 使用BMO分析和多维BSDE比较定理证明这些BSDE解的存在性；3. 通过将成本泛函重新表述为二次泛函与候选值函数之和，证明最优控制问题的适定性；4. 在附加假设下获得唯一最优策略。

Result: 1. 证明了具有无界系数的线性BSDE系统解的存在性（文献中首次出现）；2. 建立了最优控制问题的适定性（值函数有限且最优策略存在时唯一）；3. 在附加假设下获得了唯一最优策略；4. 通过BSDE的适定性结果实现了成本泛函的重新表述。

Conclusion: 成功解决了无限时域下具有半鞅策略、随机系数和状态转换的最优控制问题，建立了相关BSDE系统的适定性理论，为这类复杂随机控制问题提供了系统的分析框架和求解方法。

Abstract: We study an optimal control problem on infinite time horizon with semimartingale strategies, random coefficients and regime switching. The value function and the optimal strategy can be characterized in terms of three systems of backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) with infinite horizon. One of them is a system of linear BSDEs with unbounded coefficients and infinite horizon, which seems to be new in literature. We establish the existence of the solutions to these BSDEs by BMO analysis and comparison theorem for multi-dimensional BSDEs. Next, we establish that the optimal control problem is well posed, in the sense that the value function is finite and the optimal strategy-when it exists-is unique. This is achieved by reformulating the cost functional as the sum of a quadratic functional and the candidate value function. The reformulation crucially relies on the well-established well-posedness results for systems of BSDEs. Finally, under additional assumptions, we obtain the unique optimal strategy.

</details>


### [127] [Convergent Lifted Lasserre Hierarchy of SDPs for Minimizing Expectation of Piecewise Polynomial Loss over Wasserstein Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20660)
*N. D. Dizon,Q. Y. Huang,T. D. Chuong,G. Li,V. Jeyakumar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Wasserstein球上分段多项式损失函数期望的最小化问题，建立了半定规划松弛层次结构的渐近收敛性，并提出了新的提升正性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机源于分布鲁棒优化问题中经常出现的关键子问题——在Wasserstein球上最小化分段多项式损失函数的期望。这类无限维优化问题在实际应用中广泛存在，但缺乏有效的数值求解框架。

Method: 方法包括：1）建立半定规划松弛层次结构来近似原优化问题；2）开发新的提升正性证书，证明在Archimedean基本半代数集上的正分段多项式具有结构化的平方和表示；3）在定义多项式为凸的适当条件下，证明所提层次结构具有有限收敛性。

Result: 主要结果：1）证明了SDP松弛层次结构的渐近收敛性；2）建立了分段多项式在Archimedean基本半代数集上的结构化平方和表示理论；3）在凸多项式条件下获得了有限收敛性；4）通过收益估计和投资组合优化的数值实验验证了方法的实用性和普适性。

Conclusion: 结论：本文为Wasserstein球上分段多项式期望最小化问题提供了一个理论严谨且计算可行的框架，通过SDP松弛层次结构和新的正性证书理论，有效解决了这类无限维优化问题的近似求解，并在实际应用中展示了良好的性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates the minimization of the expectation of piecewise polynomial loss functions over Wasserstein balls. This optimization problem often appears as a key sub-problem of distributionally robust optimization problems. We establish the asymptotic convergence of a hierarchy of semi-definite programming (SDP) relaxations, providing a framework for approximating the optimal values of these inherently infinite-dimensional optimization problems. A central foundational contribution is the development of a new lifted positivity certificate: we demonstrate that piecewise polynomials positive over Archimedean basic semi-algebraic sets admit a structured system of sum-of-squares (SOS) representations. Furthermore, we prove that the proposed hierarchy achieves finite convergence under suitable conditions when the defining polynomials are convex. The practical utility and versatility of this approach are demonstrated via numerical experiments in revenue estimation and portfolio optimization.

</details>


### [128] [Implicit Decision Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20793)
*Isaac Rudich,Louis-Martin Rousseau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出隐式决策图(implicit Decision Diagrams)，通过隐式存储边将每层复杂度从O(w²)降至O(w)，并证明这是最优复杂度，开发了Julia求解器ImplicitDDs.jl


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策图在离散优化中应用广泛，但构建复杂度高：限制决策图每层需要O(w log w)，松弛决策图需要O(w²)，其中w是宽度。增加宽度能提高边界质量但代价是时间和内存消耗增加。现有研究关注DD的边界理论，但DD构建本身的复杂度问题被忽视。

Method: 引入隐式决策图，将边隐式存储而非显式存储，从而将每层复杂度降低到O(w)。证明这是最优复杂度：任何将状态更新和合并操作视为黑盒的框架都无法做得更好。开发了开源Julia求解器ImplicitDDs.jl，利用理论实现的实现优化。

Result: 隐式决策图将限制决策图和松弛决策图的每层复杂度都降至O(w)，这是理论最优。实验表明，基于隐式DD的求解器在子集和问题上表现优于Gurobi。

Conclusion: 隐式决策图通过隐式存储边实现了最优复杂度，将挑战从算法开销转移到底层工程实现。开发的ImplicitDDs.jl求解器展示了该方法的实际优势，为决策图在优化中的应用提供了更高效的实现框架。

Abstract: Decision Diagrams (DDs) have emerged as a powerful tool for discrete optimization, with rapidly growing adoption. DDs are directed acyclic layered graphs; restricted DDs are a generalized greedy heuristic for finding feasible solutions, and relaxed DDs compute combinatorial relaxed bounds. There is substantial theory that leverages DD-based bounding, yet the complexity of constructing the DDs themselves has received little attention. Standard restricted DD construction requires $O(w \log(w))$ per layer; standard relaxed DD construction requires $O(w^2)$, where $w$ is the width of the DD. Increasing $w$ improves bound quality at the cost of more time and memory.
  We introduce implicit Decision Diagrams, storing arcs implicitly rather than explicitly, and reducing per-layer complexity to $O(w)$ for restricted and relaxed DDs. We prove this is optimal: any framework treating state-update and merge operations as black boxes cannot do better.
  Optimal complexity shifts the challenge from algorithmic overhead to low-level engineering. We show how implicit DDs can drive a MIP solver, and release ImplicitDDs.jl, an open-source Julia solver exploiting the implementation refinements our theory enables. Experiments demonstrate the solver outperforms Gurobi on Subset Sum.

</details>


### [129] [A subdifferential characterization via Busemann functions and applications to DC optimization on Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20931)
*O. P. Ferreira,D. S. Gonçalves,M. S. Louzeiro,S. Z. Németh,J. Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Busemann函数的Hadamard流形上凸函数次微分的新的刻画方法，并利用其凹性设计Riemannian差凸(DC)优化算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hadamard流形框架中，次梯度通过逆指数映射提供的全局下界模型可能缺乏有用的凸性或凹性结构，限制了Riemannian优化算法的设计

Method: 利用Busemann函数的关键性质提出新的次微分刻画方法，获得凹边界函数；基于此设计Hadamard流形上的差凸(DC)优化方法，包括Riemannian DC算法(DCA)的重新表述

Result: 提出的Busemann DCA产生测地凸子问题，相比经典Riemannian DCA具有优势；通过初步数值实验验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: Busemann函数为Hadamard流形上的次微分刻画提供了新视角，其凹性特性使得能够设计更有效的Riemannian DC优化算法，在Riemannian优化领域具有应用潜力

Abstract: This paper investigates the properties of Busemann functions on Hadamard manifolds and their use in optimization algorithms in Riemannian settings. We present a new Busemann-based characterization of the subdifferential, which is particularly well suited to Riemannian optimization. In the classical Hadamard manifold framework, a subgradient provides a global lower model of a convex function expressed through the inverse exponential map. However, this model may fail to exhibit a useful convexity or concavity structure. By contrast, our characterization yields a concave bounding function by exploiting key properties of Busemann functions. We use this concavity to design and analyze difference-of-convex (DC) optimization methods on Hadamard manifolds. In particular, we reformulate the classical DC algorithm (DCA) for Riemannian contexts and study its convergence properties. We also report preliminary numerical experiments comparing the proposed Busemann DCA, which leads to geodesically convex subproblems, with the classical Riemannian DCA.

</details>


### [130] [Asymptotics of solutions to the linear search problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20991)
*Robin A. Heinonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文获得了对称线性搜索问题解的精确主导渐近行为，适用于具有单调、充分规则尾部的任意正概率密度函数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对称线性搜索问题解的渐近行为，为具有特定尾部性质的任意概率密度函数提供精确的渐近分析

Method: 使用渐近分析方法，针对具有单调且充分规则尾部的正概率密度函数，推导对称线性搜索问题解的精确主导渐近项

Result: 获得了对称线性搜索问题解的精确主导渐近表达式，适用于实线上任意具有单调规则尾部的正概率密度，类似结果也适用于紧区间上的密度函数

Conclusion: 该研究为对称线性搜索问题的渐近行为提供了精确的理论结果，扩展了现有渐近分析的适用范围

Abstract: The exact leading asymptotics of solutions to the symmetric linear search problem are obtained for any positive probability density on the real line with a monotonic, sufficiently regular tail. A similar result holds for densities on a compact interval.

</details>


### [131] [Robustness certificates in data-driven non-convex optimization with additively-uncertain constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21090)
*Alexander J Gallo,Massimiliano Zoggia,Alessandro Falsone,Maria Prandini,Simone Garatti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有非凸约束的鲁棒优化问题，提出基于场景鲁棒优化的高效概率鲁棒性证书方法，特别适用于约束具有加性不确定结构的决策问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非凸优化问题中约束包含加性不确定项的情况，传统场景鲁棒优化方法计算成本高。作者旨在利用约束结构特性，开发计算效率更高的概率鲁棒性保证方法。

Method: 利用约束中不确定性的加性结构（独立于决策变量），提出先验和后验分布无关的概率鲁棒性证书方法。基于这些结果，开发一次性确定和增量确定数据集大小的两种方法，后验评估和增量方法无需求解非凸场景规划。

Result: 在真实数据的机组组合问题上进行对比分析，结果显示相对于通用非凸问题的场景理论方法，该方法在保守性增加有限的情况下实现了显著的计算节省。

Conclusion: 对于具有加性不确定结构的非凸优化问题，该方法能够以最小计算成本提供概率鲁棒性保证，在保持鲁棒性的同时大幅提升计算效率，为实际工程应用提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We consider decision-making problems that are formulated as non-convex optimization programs where uncertainty enters the constraints through an additive term, independent of the decision variables, and robustness is imposed using a finite data-set, according to the scenario robust optimization paradigm. By exploiting the structure of the constraints, we show that both a priori and a posteriori distribution-free probabilistic robustness certificates for a possibly sub-optimal solution to the resulting data-driven optimization problem can be obtained with minimal computational effort. Building on these results, we also discuss a one-shot and an incremental procedure to determine the size of the data-set so as to guarantee a user-chosen robustness level. Notably, both the a posteriori robustness assessment and incremental data-set sizing do not require to solve the non-convex scenario program. A comparative analysis performed on the unit commitment problem using real data reveals a limited increase in conservativeness with a significant computational saving with respect to the application of scenario theory results for general, non necessarily structured, non-convex problems.

</details>


### [132] [Complexity of Classical Acceleration for $\ell_1$-Regularized PageRank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21138)
*Kimon Fountoulakis,David Martínez-Rubio*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 分析FISTA算法计算ℓ₁正则化PageRank的度加权工作量，研究加速方法能否将α依赖从1/α改进为1/√α，同时保持1/ρ的局部性缩放


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究加速近端梯度方法（FISTA）在计算ℓ₁正则化PageRank时的性能，探索是否能在保持局部性缩放1/ρ的同时，将α依赖从1/α改进为1/√α

Method: 对轻微过正则化目标函数分析FISTA算法，在可检查的约束条件下，证明所有虚假激活都保持在边界集B内，得到包含加速项(ρ√α)⁻¹log(α/ε)和边界开销√vol(B)/(ρα³/²)的界

Result: 在度加权工作量模型下，实验显示合成图和真实图上存在加速和减速两种状态，提供了保证约束条件的图结构条件

Conclusion: FISTA算法可以在特定约束条件下实现ℓ₁正则化PageRank计算的加速，但存在边界开销，需要满足图结构条件来保证约束

Abstract: We study the degree-weighted work required to compute $\ell_1$-regularized PageRank using the standard one-gradient-per-iteration accelerated proximal-gradient method (FISTA). For non-accelerated local methods, the best known worst-case work scales as $\widetilde{O} ((αρ)^{-1})$, where $α$ is the teleportation parameter and $ρ$ is the $\ell_1$-regularization parameter. A natural question is whether FISTA can improve the dependence on $α$ from $1/α$ to $1/\sqrtα$ while preserving the $1/ρ$ locality scaling. The challenge is that acceleration can break locality by transiently activating nodes that are zero at optimality, thereby increasing the cost of gradient evaluations. We analyze FISTA on a slightly over-regularized objective and show that, under a checkable confinement condition, all spurious activations remain inside a boundary set $\mathcal{B}$. This yields a bound consisting of an accelerated $(ρ\sqrtα)^{-1}\log(α/\varepsilon)$ term plus a boundary overhead $\sqrt{vol(\mathcal{B})}/(ρα^{3/2})$. We provide graph-structural conditions that imply such confinement. Experiments on synthetic and real graphs show the resulting speedup and slowdown regimes under the degree-weighted work model.

</details>
